Field guide/Skill node reference

Every skill, in one place.

588 configured nodes. One point each. Search the actual descriptions before spending in game.

This is a read-only reference. Optional integration effects and scripted conditions follow the installed server. Read the skill guide →

Potion LongevityAlchemist · alchemist_class1 POINT

+5% duration of non-instant beneficial potions you drink; capped at +50%.
Iron's Spells: +3% base mana regeneration.
Ars Nouveau: +0.3 mana regeneration perk.

Alchemist | 1 skill point. Removed on respec. Optional mod bonuses apply only when that mod is installed. Modernized from the old tree; see the conversion catalogue.
